These interactions naturally unfold over multiple turns, a pattern now mirrored in interactions with LLMs. Yet current evaluation frameworks do not capture model behavior in these settings, where misconceptions can emerge, persist, or evolve over the course of a conversation. Whether LLMs can reliably correct such misconceptions over time remains largely unexamined. To study this, we introduce ThReadMed-QA, a multi-turn medical dialogue dataset of 2,437 patient-physician conversation threads comprising 8,204 question-answer pairs, derived from real patient interactions on AskDocs. This dataset enables systematic evaluation of whether models can detect and correct misconceptions under a multi-turn context. We evaluate five LLMs using a rubric-based LLM-as-a-Judge framework that scores responses based on their ability to identify and correct misconceptions. Our experiments reveal a consistent pattern: even frontier models that can address misconceptions in a single interaction degrade substantially over subsequent turns. GPT-5 and Claude-Haiku correct these false presuppositions around 85% on initial questions but drop to roughly 50% within two follow-ups. An oracle analysis replacing prior model outputs with physician responses shows that much of the degradation is driven by error propagation, while performance remains imperfect even under correct context. Even when models tend to correct misconceptions initially, their performance degrades substantially over later turns, leading to inconsistent and potentially unsafe guidance in patient-facing settings and highlighting the need for evaluation frameworks that capture multi-turn behavior.

</details>


### [140] [LLM Judges Can Be Too Generous When There Is No Reference Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12885)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti,Sowmya Vajjala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究无参考答案场景下 LLM 评审器是否可靠，发现其容易高估错误答案；加入参考答案会显著改变判断，并更接近人类标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式模型回答常用 LLM 作为评审器，且很多评估没有标准参考答案。论文关注的问题是：在缺少参考答案时，LLM 评审器是否真正具备可靠判断能力，以及如何在使用前校准其可靠性。

Method: 作者提出两阶段流程：第一阶段进行校准实验，评估评审模型是否具备所评任务的相关知识；第二阶段进行敏感性实验，考察提示词中是否加入参考答案、以及参考答案位置变化，会如何影响评审模型的判断。实验覆盖三种语言，并与部分人类标注进行比较。

Result: 实验显示，在没有参考答案时，被评估的 LLM 评审器倾向于给错误答案过高评价；加入参考答案信息后，在某些设置中正确/错误判断最多可发生 85% 的翻转。这些由参考答案引发的变化通常与人类判断一致。

Conclusion: 论文认为，在无参考评估中直接使用 LLM 评审器存在明显风险。研究者和实践者应先使用包含参考答案的样本对评审器进行校准，再将其用于无参考答案场景；文中方法也为其他任务中的 LLM 评审器校准提供了可复用框架。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly being used to evaluate open-ended model responses, often in no-reference settings where a ground-truth answer is unavailable. However, can they reliably assess in such evaluation setups? We explore this question in this paper through a two stage pipeline with a) calibration experiments that assess the judge model's knowledge of the task it is evaluating, and b) sensitivity experiments that assess how the judge model's performance is impacted by the presence and positioning of the reference answer in the prompt. Across experiments covering three languages, we show that the judge models we evaluated tend to over-credit incorrect answers in the absence of a reference answer, and adding reference answer information to the prompt flips the judge model's correct/incorrect decisions by as much as 85% in some experimental settings. Comparison with a subset of human annotations shows that these reference-driven changes generally align with human judgments. Our results emphasize the need for calibrating the LLM judges with a sample with reference-aware evaluation before using them in reference-free setups reliably, and our methodology provides a blueprint for researchers and practitioners in doing such calibration of LLM judges for other tasks.

</details>


### [141] [The Illusion of Robustness: Aggregate Accuracy Hides Prediction Flips under Task-Irrelevant Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12963)
*Yanzhe Zhang,Sanmi Koyejo,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出，LLM在加入任务无关长上下文后总体准确率看似稳定，但单个样本预测会出现显著不稳定，形成被平均指标掩盖的尾部风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中，LLM常面对包含大量部分无关信息的长上下文；仅用总体准确率评估可能无法发现上下文对个别样本可靠性的影响，因此需要研究上下文诱发的逐样本风险。

Method: 作者在受控实验中将任务无关上下文前置到基准问题中，并使用不同模型、数据集、上下文类型与长度进行测试；还考察了无语义伪词、测试时计算量以及模型开发阶段对预测稳定性的影响。

Result: 总体准确率变化很小，显示表面鲁棒性；但在少量样本上，即使随机字符组成的无意义伪词也会显著改变预测，既可能降低也可能提升性能。这种双向效应在多模型、多数据集上稳定存在，但受影响样本大多具有模型特异性。

Conclusion: LLM存在由上下文引发的逐样本不稳定性和尾部风险，传统聚合准确率会掩盖这些问题；评估LLM可靠性时应引入逐样本层面的稳定性与风险分析。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow more capable, they are increasingly deployed in context-rich settings where task inputs are often accompanied by long, partially irrelevant context. In a controlled setting, we find that state-of-the-art models often appear robust to task-irrelevant context at the aggregate level: prepending it to benchmark questions causes little change in overall accuracy. This aggregate stability, however, masks significant per-example instability. Even semantically meaningless pseudo-words, formed by randomly combining characters, can markedly shift model predictions on a small fraction of examples, degrading performance on some while improving it on others. This two-sided effect holds consistently across a wide range of models and datasets, yet the affected examples are largely model-specific. We further show that this instability is modulated by context type, context length, test-time compute, and model development stage. Together, our findings reveal context-induced tail risks concealed by aggregate accuracy, motivating per-example reliability evaluation of language models.

</details>


### [142] [PalmClaw: A Native On-Device Agent Framework for Mobile Phones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13027)
*Hongru Cai,Yongqi Li,Ran Wei,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PalmClaw 是一个在手机端原生运行的开源 LLM Agent 框架，将移动设备能力封装为带显式参数、结构化结果和清晰执行边界的工具，相比 GUI 操作型移动 Agent 提升任务成功率并显著缩短完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动 Agent 多依赖点击、滑动、输入等 GUI 操作，导致操作序列长、强依赖界面变化、难以直接调用设备能力，并且执行边界不清晰；而移动设备又具有广泛可用性，承载用户数据、传感器和常用应用，因此需要更原生、更可控的移动端 Agent 框架。

Method: 提出 PalmClaw 框架，在移动手机本地管理会话、记忆、技能、工具和 Agent 循环；将设备能力抽象为 device tools，每个工具具有明确参数、结构化返回结果和清晰执行边界，使 Agent 能直接、显式、受控地使用移动端能力。

Result: 实验显示，相比最强基线，PalmClaw 的任务成功率相对提升 11.5%，任务完成时间减少 94.9%，同时降低了配置负担，并通过执行轨迹展示了执行边界的应用方式。

Conclusion: PalmClaw 证明了将移动设备能力工具化、结构化并在端侧原生运行 Agent，可以比传统 GUI 操作式移动 Agent 更高效、更可靠且更易控制，适合作为移动端 LLM Agent 的基础框架。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have moved beyond generating responses to executing multi-step tasks by calling tools, observing the results, and iteratively deciding the next action. Most agent systems run on desktops or servers, which support tool use and task automation. Mobile devices are also important agent environments because they are widely accessible and contain users' data, sensors, and daily-use applications. Existing mobile agents mainly operate smartphones through graphical user interface (GUI) actions such as tapping, swiping, and typing, which often form long, interface-dependent sequences, cannot directly access device capabilities, and make execution boundaries difficult to define. We present \textbf{PalmClaw}, an open-source agent framework that runs natively on mobile phones and manages the sessions, memory, skills, tools, and agent loop directly on the device. PalmClaw exposes device capabilities as device tools with explicit arguments, structured results, and clearly defined execution boundaries. This design enables agents to use mobile capabilities directly while keeping each action explicit and controlled. Experiments show an 11.5\% relative improvement in task success and a 94.9\% reduction in completion time over the strongest baseline, with lower setup burden and traces illustrating how execution boundaries are applied. Code is available at https://github.com/ModalityDance/PalmClaw.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Cyclic codes over a commutative non-unitary ring of order 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11965)
*Marvin Olavides,Jon-Lark Kim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究 Fine 分类中的 4 阶交换非幺环 I₂ 上的循环码，借助二元剩余码与挠码刻画其结构，并建立其 Gray 像与二元拟循环码的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非域、非幺环上的编码理论可产生具有特殊代数结构的码类，但 I₂ 上循环码的结构、对偶性以及与二元码之间的关系尚需系统刻画。

Method: 作者通过引入 twisted 与 untwisted 循环码，利用 residue/torsion codes over F₂ 分析 I₂ 上码的循环性；用 twist map 与 cyclic shift 的相容条件给出判别；进一步通过 Gray 映射研究其二元像，并考察对偶与短长度分类。

Result: 证明 I₂ 上循环码的 Gray 像是 index 2 的二元拟循环码；给出循环性的相容性刻画；证明循环码的对偶仍为循环码；并完成 n≤7 时置换不等价循环码的分类及相关结构性质确定。

Conclusion: 论文建立了 I₂ 上循环码的较完整结构理论，揭示其与二元拟循环码之间的桥梁，并为小长度码的分类与后续构造提供了基础。

Abstract: Let $I_2$ be the commutative non-unitary ring of order $4$ arising in the classification of Fine. In this paper, we investigate cyclic codes over $I_2$ through their associated residue and torsion codes over $\mathbb{F}_2$. We introduce the notions of twisted and untwisted cyclic codes and characterize cyclicity in terms of a compatibility condition involving the twist map and the cyclic shift. Connections between cyclic codes over $I_2$ and binary quasi-cyclic codes are established via Gray maps. In particular, we show that the Gray image of a cyclic code over $I_2$ is a binary quasi-cyclic code of index $2$. We also study duality properties of cyclic codes over $I_2$ and prove that the dual of a cyclic code is again cyclic. Finally, we classify permutation inequivalent cyclic codes over $I_2$ for lengths $n \le 7$ and determine various structural properties of these codes.

</details>


### [144] [Generalized Segmented GRAND for Guesswork Reduction in Turbo Product Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12147)
*Lukas Rapp,Jiewei Feng,Muriel Médard,Ken R. Duffy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出 GSegGRAND，一种更通用的分段 GRAND 解码框架，可在更广泛的二进制线性码上利用更多约束，显著降低猜测复杂度，并扩展到软输出与涡轮乘积解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Segmented GRAND 通过约束噪声猜测降低解码 guesswork，但依赖特定校验矩阵结构，限制了可利用约束数量和适用码类。论文旨在突破这些结构性限制，使约束猜测能适用于更多编码并进一步降低低时延解码复杂度。

Method: 作者提出新的校验结构以及将一般码映射到该结构的变换，形成 GSegGRAND；该方法最多可结合 log2(n) 个约束进行噪声模式猜测。同时，作者将 SOGRAND 的软输出思想扩展到受约束猜测场景，推导适用于 GSegGRAND 的软输出公式，并将其用于涡轮乘积解码。

Result: 相比原 Segmented GRAND，GSegGRAND 额外减少约 75% 的 guesswork；在软输出涡轮乘积解码中，最多实现 88% 的 guesswork 降低，同时保持接近最大似然解码的性能潜力。

Conclusion: GSegGRAND 通过通用化的校验结构和软输出扩展，显著提升了 GRAND 类解码器的适用性与效率，是面向未来通信系统低时延译码的有前景方案。

Abstract: Guessing random additive noise decoding (GRAND) can efficiently decode any moderately redundant code with near maximum likelihood (ML) performance via noise effect guessing. For binary linear codes, Rowshan and Yuan's Segmented GRAND was the first to show that constrained guessing can reduce guesswork. Although powerful, their approach requires a specific parity-check matrix structure that limits the number of constraints that can be exploited as well as the class of applicable codes. Here we introduce GSegGRAND, a generalization of Segmented GRAND that circumvents its limitations. Built on a novel parity check structure and a transformation that maps codes into this structure, GSegGRAND efficiently incorporates up to log2(n) constraints for a wide range of codes, reducing guesswork by an additional 75% over Segmented GRAND. To leverage that advantage for soft-output decoding, we derive an accurate soft-output (SO) equation for GSegGRAND by extending soft-output GRAND (SOGRAND) to incorporate constrained guessing. Applying this SO to turbo product decoding, GSegGRAND achieves up to 88% guesswork reduction, making it a promising candidate for low-latency decoding in future communication systems.

</details>


### [145] [Auxiliary Nodes for BP Decoding of Quantum LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12187)
*Daniel Tandler,Paul Bezner,Stephan ten Brink*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在 CSS 量子 LDPC 码解码图中引入辅助变量节点与辅助校验节点的通用框架，用于改善 BP 解码图结构，并在电路级噪声下通过图派生子码集成解码显著降低逻辑错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CSS QLDPC 码虽具有稀疏解码图、适合低复杂度 syndrome-based BP 解码，但其构造常带来短环、退化性等不利于 BP 收敛与性能的问题，因此需要一种能直接调控解码图结构的通用方法。

Method: 作者提出兼容标准稳定子测量框架的 AVN/ACN 引入机制，将辅助变量节点和辅助校验节点加入 CSS 码解码图；并说明 4-cycle removal 与 subcode ensemble decoding 可视为该框架的特例。在此基础上进一步设计 graph-derived subcode ensemble decoder，并分析 BP 迭代次数和校验节点消息缩放对 4 环移除收益的影响。

Result: 研究发现，4-cycle removal 的性能增益强烈依赖 BP 迭代次数与 check-node message scaling；所提出的图派生子码集成解码器在电路级噪声模型下，相比对应的无 4 环解码图上的 BP，显著降低了每轮逻辑错误率。

Conclusion: 该框架为 CSS QLDPC 码的解码图设计提供了额外自由度，可系统化解释并扩展已有图改造/集成解码技术；通过合理构造辅助节点与子码集成，可有效缓解短环和退化性对 BP 的负面影响，提升量子 LDPC 码解码性能。

Abstract: Many recently proposed Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes have sparse decoding graphs, enabling syndrome-based belief propagation (BP) decoding at low complexity. Their construction, however, often results in properties that impair BP performance, such as short cycles and degeneracy. In this work, we propose a general framework for introducing auxiliary variable nodes (AVNs) and auxiliary check nodes (ACNs) into the decoding graph of CSS codes, compatible with the standard stabilizer measurement framework. This provides an additional degree of freedom in the design of the decoding graph itself and can be used to tackle the aforementioned shortcomings. We show that recently proposed techniques, 4-cycle removal and subcode ensemble decoding, can be interpreted as instances of this framework. For 4-cycle removal, we find that the gains depend strongly on the BP iteration count and check-node message scaling. Building on this framework, we further propose a graph-derived subcode ensemble decoder and demonstrate under circuit-level noise that it substantially reduces the per-round logical error rate compared with BP on the corresponding 4-cycle-free decoding graph.

</details>


### [146] [Quantum Codes from $r$-Nearly Self-Orthogonal Linear Codes via Jordan Canonical Form over $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12242)
*Liangdong Lu,Ruipan Yang,Yang Liu,Qiang Fu,Guanmin Guo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于 Jordan 标准形的构造框架，可从任意 \(\mathbb F_{q^2}\) 上经典线性码构造 \(q\)-元量子稳定子码，无需经典码满足 Hermitian 自正交/包含对偶条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子稳定子码构造通常要求经典线性码满足对偶包含或 Hermitian 自正交条件，这限制了可用经典码的范围。本文希望放宽该限制，使任意经典线性码也能系统地转化为量子码，并尽可能保持距离参数。

Method: 给定经典码 \(\mathcal C=[n,k,d]_{q^2}\) 及校验矩阵 \(H\)，用 \(r=\mathrm{Rank}(HH^\dagger)\) 衡量其偏离 Hermitian 自正交的程度。对 Hermitian 内积矩阵 \(A=HH^\dagger\) 作 Jordan 分解 \(A=PJ_AP^{-1}\)，沿 Jordan 基 \(W=P^{-1}\) 进行秩一扰动，逐步降低该矩阵秩，从而显式构造 Hermitian 自正交码 \(\mathcal C_{\mathrm{so}}=[n+r,n-k]_{q^2}\)。

Result: 在满足距离保持充分条件时，得到量子稳定子码参数 \([[n+r,2k-n+r,\ge d]]_q\)。将该构造应用于若干经典码后，得到若干刷新或补充 Grassl 表中最佳已知参数的量子码。

Conclusion: 该框架将量子稳定子码构造从严格自正交约束扩展到任意经典线性码，通过 Jordan 标准形和秩一扰动实现可控的自正交化，并在实际参数上展现出改进已知量子码表的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce a Jordan-canonical-form framework for constructing $q$-ary quantum stabilizer codes from arbitrary classical linear codes over $\F_{q^2}$. The framework does not require the classical linear code $\mathcal{C}$ to satisfy the dual-containing condition (i.e., self-orthogonality). Given a classical code $\mathcal{C}=[n,k,d]_{q^2}$ with parity-check matrix $H$, we measure the obstruction to Hermitian self-orthogonality by the rank $r=(n-k)-\dim_{\F_{q^2}}(\mathcal{C}^{\perp_h}\cap \mathcal{C})$. The ingredient code $\mathcal{C}$ is $r$-nearly dual containing, or, equivalently, $\mathcal{C}^{\perp_h}$ is $r$-nearly self-orthogonal, by which we mean that $r=\Rank(HH^{\dagger})=\dim_{\F_{q^2}}(\mathcal{C}^{\perp_h})-\dim_{\F_{q^2}}(\mathcal{C}^{\perp_h}\cap \mathcal{C})$. By systematically reducing the rank of the Hermitian inner-product matrix $A=HH^{\dagger}$ through rank-one perturbations along the Jordan basis $W=P^{-1}$ of the decomposition $A=PJ_AP^{-1}$, we construct an explicit Hermitian self-orthogonal code $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{so}}=[n+r,n-k]_{q^2}$. A sufficient distance-preservation criterion guarantees that the resulting $q$-ary quantum code has parameters $[[n+r,2k-n+r,\geq d]]_q$. Applying this construction to classical codes produces several record quantum codes that improve or supplement the best-known parameters in Grassl's tables.

</details>


### [147] [Metasurface Antenna-Enabled LEO Satellite Constellation Communications: Design and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12328)
*Wenfei Yao,Xiaoming Chen,Qi Wang,Qiao Qi,Ming Ying*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出在低轨卫星上采用超表面天线，通过联合馈源天线-地面用户调度与超表面无源波束成形来提升加权和速率，并用AO框架结合MCMF与WMMSE-SDR求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代低轨卫星星座面临频谱效率不足与星载硬件复杂度高的瓶颈。传统数字/混合波束成形可能带来较高射频链路和计算开销，因此需要一种能在波域直接实现高精度波束控制、同时抑制多用户干扰的低复杂度架构。

Method: 论文引入超表面集成馈源天线架构，构建以加权和速率最大化为目标的联合优化问题，变量包括馈源天线到地面用户的调度以及超表面无源波束成形。针对该混合整数非线性规划问题，提出交替优化算法：调度子问题使用多项式时间的最小费用最大流方法求最优匹配；波束成形子问题采用加权最小均方误差方法并结合半定松弛技术求解。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的超表面天线赋能低轨卫星通信方案和联合调度/波束成形算法能够有效提升系统性能，验证了其在低轨卫星星座通信中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，超表面天线可作为降低星载硬件复杂度并提升频谱效率的有前景方案；通过将组合调度优化与连续波束成形优化分解处理，所提AO算法能有效应对MA赋能LEO系统中的WSR最大化问题。

Abstract: Next-generation low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations face critical bottlenecks in spectral efficiency and onboard hardware complexity. To overcome these limitations, this paper introduces a novel architecture enabled by metasurface antennas (MAs) at the LEO satellites. In particular, MAs are metasurface-integrated feed antennas that perform high-precision beamforming directly in the wave domain, thereby effectively mitigating multi-user interference. Based on such an antenna architecture, a weighted sum rate (WSR) maximization problem is formulated by jointly optimizing the scheduling of feed antennas to terrestrial users (TUs) and the passive beamforming of the metasurface for system performance enhancement. To address this mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) challenge, an alternating optimization (AO)-based joint scheduling and beamforming algorithm is proposed. On the one hand, the proposed algorithm incorporates a polynomial-time minimum-cost maximum-flow (MCMF) method, which is dedicated to the optimal scheduling of feed antennas and TUs. On the other hand, it adopts a weighted minimum mean square error (WMMSE) method integrated with semidefinite relaxation (SDR) technique, which is tailored for metasurface beamforming design. Simulation results confirm the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm for MA-enabled LEO satellite constellation communications.

</details>


### [148] [Robust Design of Integrated Sensing and Communication in LEO Satellite Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12337)
*Hezhen Yang,Xiaoming Chen,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究低轨卫星系统中的通感一体化（ISAC），提出一种鲁棒波束成形算法，在同频同时服务通信用户并感知多个目标的条件下，最小化卫星发射功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星感知与通信需求增长，但频谱和轨道资源有限；将感知与通信集成到单颗卫星中可提升资源利用效率。同时，卫星载荷能量受限，且信道相位不确定会加剧感知与通信之间的干扰，因此需要鲁棒、低功耗的设计。

Method: 建立LEO卫星ISAC框架，使卫星在同一频谱上同时感知多个目标并服务多个通信用户；在信道相位不确定条件下，以最小化总发射功率为目标，联合满足感知均方误差（MSE）约束和通信信干噪比（SINR）约束，设计鲁棒波束成形算法。

Result: 理论分析表明所提算法适用于LEO卫星ISAC场景，并能有效处理由相位不确定性引起的跨功能干扰；大量仿真显示该算法相较基线方法具有更优性能。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在LEO卫星中部署ISAC的可行性，并表明鲁棒波束成形可在保障感知与通信性能的同时降低发射功率，从而节省频谱、轨道与能量资源。

Abstract: With the growing demand for satellite sensing and communication, the limited wireless resources are difficult to support multiple satellite systems. Therefore, it is desired to investigate integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) in low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite systems to enable multi-functionality within a single satellite, thereby saving both spectrum and orbital resources. In this paper, a framework for ISAC in LEO satellite systems is established, where a satellite can simultaneously sense multiple targets and serve multiple communication users (CUs) over the same spectrum. Considering the limited onboard energy of satellite, a novel robust beamforming design algorithm is developed with the goal of minimizing total transmit power while satisfying the mean squared error (MSE) requirements for sensing and signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) requirements for communication in presence of channel phase uncertainty which exacerbates the cross-functional interference. According to theoretical analysis, the proposed algorithm for ISAC in LEO satellite systems is effective. Moreover, extensive simulations confirm the superiority of the proposed algorithm over baselines.

</details>


### [149] [A Superposition Signaling Scheme for Integrated Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12549)
*Gökhan Yılmaz,Hamdi Joudeh,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究OFDM一体化感知与通信系统在发射信号积分旁瓣电平作为输入代价时的信道容量，并提出一种接近容量的简单信令方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一体化感知与通信系统需要在感知性能与通信速率之间权衡。积分旁瓣电平影响感知质量，因此有必要从信息论角度刻画在功率与感知相关约束下的通信容量及可实现信令设计。

Method: 作者将原问题化简为带功率约束与峰度约束的复加性白高斯噪声信道容量问题；分析感知最优与通信最优输入分布，并刻画中间区域容量达成输入的离散幅度、多同心环结构；进一步提出将常模输入与复高斯输入叠加的可调信令策略，并通过连续译码给出其精确速率表达式。

Result: 感知最优点对应圆对称常模输入，通信最优点对应复高斯输入；两者之间容量达成输入具有离散幅度支撑和同心多环几何结构。数值结果显示，所提叠加策略与容量的最坏情况差距约为0.043 bits。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了OFDM一体化感知通信中感知代价约束下容量问题的清晰信息论刻画，并表明简单的极端输入叠加方案即可在很小容量损失下实现感知-通信可调折中。

Abstract: We investigate the capacity of an integrated sensing and communication system operating with orthogonal frequency division multiplexing, where the integrated sidelobe level of the transmit signal is adopted as an input cost. The problem is reduced to the capacity of a complex additive white Gaussian noise channel under power and kurtosis constraints. The sensing-optimal and communication-optimal operating points are characterized by circularly symmetric constant-modulus and complex Gaussian inputs, respectively, while the capacity-achieving input in between has discrete amplitude support with a concentric multi-ring geometry. We propose the superposition of the two extremal inputs as a simple and tunable signaling strategy, whose rate admits an exact expression via successive decoding. Numerical results show that the proposed strategy attains a worst-case gap of approximately 0.043 bits to the capacity.

</details>


### [150] [FM-Receiver: A Foundation Model Enabled Unified Inner and Outer Neural Receiver Towards AI-Native Wireless Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12555)
*Tianyue Zheng,Chao Jiang,Linglong Dai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出 FM-Receiver：一种基于基础模型的统一神经接收机，将外接收机与内接收机整合到单一 AI-native 框架中，直接由接收信号恢复发送比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经接收机通常只在外接收机部分使用深度学习，而内接收机仍依赖传统信道解码，导致外内模块难以联合优化，也限制了高效、统一的 AI-native 无线接收机设计。

Method: 论文提出 FM-enabled unified neural receiver。核心方法包括：设计分组纠错码 Transformer，用于符号级信道解码，从而实现内外接收机的无缝融合；构建端到端 FM-Receiver，以接收信号作为基础模型输入并输出恢复比特；进一步设计三阶段配置自适应预训练策略，以提升模型对不同系统配置与场景的泛化能力。

Result: 大量仿真表明，FM-Receiver 在不同系统配置下优于基线方法，并且对未见过的频段和场景展现出较强的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了基础模型可用于构建统一的端到端神经无线接收机，有助于突破传统模块化接收机的联合优化瓶颈，并为 AI-native 通信接收机设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: With the development of artificial intelligence (AI) techniques, neural receivers, which apply AI to improve wireless receivers have been developed. However, most existing neural receivers apply deep learning only to the outer receiver while retaining conventional channel decoding for the inner receiver, which prevents joint optimization and makes it difficult to build efficient and unified AI-native receivers. To address this issue, we propose a foundation model (FM)-enabled unified neural receiver, FM-Receiver, that integrates the outer and inner receivers into a single AI-native framework, by leveraging the strong representation capability of FMs. Specifically, we introduce a grouped error correction code Transformer that performs symbol-level channel decoding, enabling seamless integration of the inner and outer receiver. Building on this, we illustrate the proposed FM-Receiver, that directly takes the received signals as input of FM and outputs the recovered transmitted bits. In addition, a three-stage configuration-adaptive pre-training strategy is designed to improve the generalization ability to diverse system configurations and scenarios. Extensive simulations show that the proposed FM-Receiver achieves better performance than baselines across different system configurations. It also demonstrates strong zero-shot generalization to unseen frequency bands and scenarios.

</details>


### [151] [Switched-Feed Pinching-Antenna Systems for Wideband Terahertz Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12646)
*Wei Jiang,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文指出太赫兹频段下PASS的波导内损耗不可忽略，并提出统一传播模型与SF-PASS架构以提升频谱效率、降低硬件成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PASS分析通常认为低频和毫米波频段中的波导内衰减可以忽略，但在太赫兹频段，可实现波导的损耗显著增大，导致既有模型和系统设计结论不再适用。因此需要建立面向THz-PASS的更准确传播模型，并探索降低损耗影响的新型架构。

Method: 论文构建统一的宽带THz-PASS传播模型，联合考虑波导内衰减、大气吸收、分子再辐射噪声和波束斜视效应；进一步推导闭式结果，包括带平均相干因子、满足带边SINR均衡条件的簇中心放置方法、放置反演阈值，以及带闭式插入损耗收益阈值的Switched-Feed PASS架构。

Result: 数值评估显示，在最适合PASS的太赫兹工作点，SF-PASS相比单馈PASS在频谱效率上有显著提升，并且在硬件成本明显更低的情况下可与大规模天线阵列竞争。

Conclusion: 太赫兹PASS设计必须显式纳入波导损耗等宽带传播因素；通过多段波导和中心射频开关实现馈电切换的SF-PASS能够有效缓解单馈结构的损耗问题，是一种具有成本优势和性能潜力的THz通信架构。

Abstract: The pinching-antenna system (PASS) uses dielectric particles along a low-loss waveguide as reconfigurable passive radiators. Existing analyses conclude that the in-waveguide attenuation is negligible at low frequencies and millimeter wave bands; we show this fails at terahertz (THz), where realizable waveguide losses are dramatically larger. We develop a unified wideband THz-PASS propagation model integrating in-waveguide attenuation, atmospheric absorption, molecular re-radiation noise, and beam squint. Closed-form results follow: a band-averaged coherence factor; a cluster-center placement satisfying a band-edge SINR equalization condition; an associated placement-inversion threshold; and a proposed \emph{Switched-Feed PASS} (SF-PASS) architecture in which a centrally located radio-frequency switch routes the signal among multiple waveguide segments, with a closed-form insertion-loss payoff threshold. Numerical evaluation at the best PASS-compatible THz operating point shows that SF-PASS substantially outperforms single-feed PASS in spectral efficiency and is competitive with a large-scale antenna array at much lower hardware costs.

</details>


### [152] [Pinching-Antenna-Assisted Terahertz Communications: Modeling and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12695)
*Wei Jiang,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向太赫兹频段的 Pinching Antenna Systems（THz-PASS）解析建模框架，并表明其在受限、线性部署场景中相较传统相控阵可获得更高频谱效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PASS 研究主要集中在毫米波频段，而扩展到太赫兹频段后，会出现波导内传播损耗、倏逝场耦合、分子吸收及再辐射噪声等特殊电磁效应，使传统建模假设不再适用。因此需要建立针对 THz-PASS 的统一分析模型。

Method: 论文构建了一个综合解析框架：同时纳入介质波导中的传播衰减、基于耦合模理论的倏逝耦合机制，以及太赫兹自由空间传播中的分子吸收和再辐射噪声。随后在相同传播条件下，将 THz-PASS 与传统相控阵进行对比评估。

Result: 比较结果显示，THz-PASS 能通过利用天线与用户之间的近距离部署优势提升有效频谱效率，尤其在受限空间和线性拓扑部署中表现突出。

Conclusion: THz-PASS 是一种适合太赫兹高频无线通信的灵活天线架构；准确建模其太赫兹特有传播与耦合效应是性能评估的关键，其潜在优势主要体现在近距离、受限或线性场景。

Abstract: Pinching antenna systems (PASS) employing dielectric waveguides have recently emerged as a promising flexible antenna architecture for high-frequency wireless communications. While prior work has focused primarily on millimeter-wave regimes, extending PASS to the terahertz (THz) band introduces distinct electromagnetic phenomena that invalidate conventional modeling assumptions. This paper develops the first analytical framework for THz-PASS that integrates in-waveguide propagation attenuation, evanescent coupling via coupled-mode theory, and THz-specific free-space effects including molecular absorption and its re-radiation noise. Using this model, we benchmark THz-PASS against conventional phased arrays under identical propagation scenarios. Our comparative evaluation reveals that THz-PASS achieves effective gains in spectral efficiency through proximity exploitation, making it particularly well-suited for confined and linear deployment topologies.

</details>


### [153] [Superimposed Transmission for Cooperative Cellular and Cell-Free Massive MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12709)
*Wei Jiang,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出一种面向协作蜂窝与无小区大规模 MIMO 系统的叠加传输策略，通过为近端用户额外叠加发送数据信号并结合 SIC 解码，在提升峰值频谱效率的同时维持小区边缘公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蜂窝、无小区或协作式大规模 MIMO 网络配置在系统容量、峰值频谱效率与边缘用户公平性之间存在权衡。论文希望通过更高效地利用基站与分布式接入点的联合传输能力，提升整体系统性能，同时避免牺牲远端/边缘用户体验。

Method: 将用户划分为近端用户和远端用户；基站为每个近端用户发送一个额外数据符号，并将其叠加在分布式接入点发送的信号之上；近端用户接收端采用连续干扰消除（SIC）机制，依次解码叠加的两个符号，从而获得额外传输增益。

Result: 该策略获得最高的峰值频谱效率，并在小区边缘保持公平性；相比已有网络配置，在系统容量方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 叠加传输结合 SIC 的设计能够有效增强协作蜂窝与无小区大规模 MIMO 系统的容量和频谱效率，是一种兼顾高吞吐与边缘公平性的有前景方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a superimposed transmission strategy for cooperative cellular and cell-free massive MIMO systems. By classifying users into near and far, the base station transmits an additional data symbol for each near user, superimposed on the signals from distributed access points. Successive interference cancellation is employed at near-user receivers to decode both symbols. The proposed strategy achieves the highest peak spectral efficiency while maintaining fairness at the cell edge, thereby outperforming all the existing network configurations in system capacity.

</details>


### [154] [A Survey on Code Equivalence: The State-of-the-Art and Open Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12870)
*Anna-Lena Horlemann,Abhinaba Mazumder,Michael Schaller,Violetta Weger*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文综述了代码等价问题及其变体，系统梳理相关理论结果、算法技术、困难性假设、已知攻击与开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码等价问题存在多种形式化定义与相关变体，不同算法、攻击方法和困难性假设之间关系复杂，需要一篇系统性综述来澄清研究现状，并为后续研究提供方向。

Method: 作者对已有文献进行综合性调研：比较不同问题表述之间的联系，归纳算法技术与困难性假设，系统回顾已知攻击方法，并分析其适用参数范围和局限性。

Result: 论文总结了代码等价问题领域的主要已有结果，明确了不同攻击在特定参数区间下的有效性，同时指出现有方法在若干场景中的不足。

Conclusion: 该综述为理解代码等价问题的困难性提供了结构化图景，并提出若干开放问题和未来研究方向，有助于推动该领域更深入的发展。

Abstract: In this work, we provide a comprehensive survey of the code equivalence problem and its variants. We explain the existing results, highlighting the relationships between different problem formulations, algorithmic techniques, and hardness assumptions. In addition, we systematically review known attacks, identify the parameter regimes in which they are effective, and discuss their limitations. Lastly, we outline several open problems and research directions, with the aim of clarifying the current landscape and guiding future work toward a deeper understanding of the hardness of code equivalence.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Optimal Adaptive Market Making: A Theoretical Framework for High-Yield Liquidity Provision in Perpetual Futures Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11888)
*Minmin Zeng,Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对零挂单手续费永续合约做市的严格随机最优控制框架，涵盖报价、库存与跨交易所对冲，并给出盈利区间、风险界与组合配置结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代去中心化永续合约交易所存在零 maker fee、资金费率、跨交易所对冲摩擦与库存风险等特征，传统做市模型难以完整刻画其收益来源、风险暴露和最优策略。论文旨在统一并扩展经典做市与信息不对称微观结构模型，以适配新型市场机制。

Method: 将做市商问题建模为滤过概率空间上的随机最优控制问题，控制变量包括自适应买卖价差与跨交易所库存对冲决策；在 CARA 效用下推导 HJB 方程和验证定理，并构建 PnL 分解、高 APY 区域判定、资金费率动态、鲁棒性边际、回撤概率界、Kelly 杠杆、遍历库存分布与多品种组合配置分析。

Result: 理论上得到 Master APY Formula、盈利/亏损相变条件、最优进出场阈值、跨交易所对冲三分法、参数不确定性容忍边界、指数型回撤概率上界、APY-VaR 恒等式、最优杠杆与破产边界，以及多交易对分散化饱和现象。数值实验通过 23 幅图展示了盈利与非盈利状态之间的相变。

Conclusion: 该框架系统解释了零手续费永续合约做市中的收益、风险与最优控制结构，统一并扩展了 Avellaneda-Stoikov、Gueant-Lehalle-Fernandez-Tapia 和 Glosten-Milgrom 模型，对去中心化永续合约市场的做市策略设计具有理论意义。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous theoretical framework for optimal market making in perpetual futures markets with zero maker fees. We model the market maker's problem as a stochastic optimal control problem on a filtered probability space, where the controls are adaptive bid-ask spreads and inventory hedging decisions across two exchanges. Our contributions include: (i) a PnL decomposition theorem separating revenue into spread income, adverse selection loss, inventory carrying cost, hedging friction, and funding rate exposure; (ii) the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation for the joint spread-inventory-hedging control problem under CARA utility with a verification theorem; (iii) High-APY Regime Theorems characterizing profitable regions via five dimensionless parameters, culminating in a Master APY Formula; (iv) analysis of zero-fee economics on decentralized perpetual exchanges with optimal entry-exit thresholds; (v) optimal cross-exchange hedging policies with funding rate dynamics and a hedge regime trichotomy; (vi) a robustness margin quantifying parameter uncertainty tolerance; (vii) exponential drawdown probability bounds and a universal APY-VaR identity; (viii) ergodic inventory distribution under optimal control with Bayesian adaptive estimation; (ix) Kelly-optimal leverage with ruin boundaries; and (x) multi-pair portfolio allocation with diversification saturation results. Numerical analysis with twenty-three figures reveals phase transitions between profitable and unprofitable regimes. Our framework unifies and extends the Avellaneda-Stoikov, Gueant-Lehalle-Fernandez-Tapia, and Glosten-Milgrom paradigms for modern decentralized venue microstructure.

</details>


### [156] [In-Context Reinforcement Learning under Non-Stationarity: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11906)
*A Run,Ziluo Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述非平稳情境下的上下文强化学习（ICRL），关注固定策略参数的决策模型如何通过上下文识别当前任务规则，并判断历史证据是否仍然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICRL综述多围绕预训练目标、模型架构、上下文格式、评测协议与理论机制展开，但对环境非平稳性关注不足。在非平稳环境中，历史上下文可能因奖励、转移、观测、动作接口、约束或记忆分布变化而失效、误导，或在旧机制回归时重新有用。

Method: 论文提出非平稳ICRL的定义，并将其与元强化学习、决策序列建模、检索增强强化学习、价值/模型感知ICRL以及奖励反馈智能体等方向联系起来；同时从三个问题组织文献：变化的对象、变化如何发生、变化对智能体的可观测性。

Result: 该综述形成了一个面向非平稳ICRL的分类框架，明确了上下文适应不仅是利用更多固定任务证据，而是需要同时推断当前决策规则与历史证据的有效性。

Conclusion: 非平稳ICRL是ICRL研究中的关键但相对欠充分探索的方向，未来方法需要具备识别环境变化、过滤过期上下文、利用可复现历史经验并在无测试时参数更新条件下适应新机制的能力。

Abstract: The development of decision-pretrained transformers, algorithm distillation, long-context meta-RL, and retrieval-augmented agents has renewed interest in in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL): the ability of a pretrained or fine-tuned decision model to infer latent task rules and improve future behavior from interaction context, without test-time parameter updates. This line of work asks when trial-and-error evidence, rewards, transitions, demonstrations, feedback, or retrieved experience can make learning-like computation happen inside the context window. However, existing surveys of ICRL mainly organize the field around pretraining objectives, architectures, context formats, evaluation protocols, and theoretical mechanisms, while the non-stationary setting remains comparatively underexamined. In changing environments, accumulated context is not merely more evidence about a fixed task: the reward specification, transition kernel, observation channel, action interface, constraint model, or demonstration and memory distribution can fall out of alignment with the current regime. Previously useful context can therefore become stale, misleading, or useful again when an old regime returns. We survey non-stationary ICRL as the problem of adapting through context while deployed policy parameters remain fixed: the policy must infer both the current decision rule and which parts of its accumulated evidence still support that rule. We define non-stationary ICRL, relate it to meta-RL, decision sequence modeling, retrieval-augmented RL, value- and model-aware ICRL, and reward-feedback agents, and organize the literature along three questions: what changes, how the change unfolds, and how observable the change is to the agent.

</details>


### [157] [Ontology-Amplified Distillation and Contextuality Auditing for Sovereign Enterprise Language Models: A Combined Proof-of-Mechanism and Negative-Results Method Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11948)
*Thanh Luong Tuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文整合两个FAOS相关研究：一是本地小样本的本体增强蒸馏机制验证，二是企业智能体路由的语境性审计方法。结果显示学生模型在越南金融任务上的grounding表现与前沿基线数值相同，但统计功效不足，不能证明等价或优越；语境性审计 pilot 未发现残余语境性，主要信号来自直接影响与构念耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受数据驻留规则约束的金融机构需要可在机构边界内部署、由租户拥有的语言模型；同时，企业多智能体系统需要治理诊断机制，以判断模型/智能体间表面分歧应如何处理。

Method: 研究1采用ontology-amplified distillation：将Qwen3.6-27B学生模型通过前沿教师轨迹的监督微调和基于本体的DPO适配Foundation AgenticOS ontology；训练数据为47个合成英文跨领域偏好对，并在单台Apple M5 Max本地训练。评测使用40个越南语金融领域保留任务。研究2整合Contextuality-by-Default审计，在local-Qwen运行与Gemma复现实验中计算校正后的canonical contextuality degree，并分析direct influence与construct coupling。

Result: 学生模型在40个任务中ground了36个，grounded rate为0.90，平均本体术语覆盖率r_onto为0.95，与GPT-5基线同为36/40。但配对差异95%置信区间跨越±4个任务，无法证明统计等价，也未验证预注册的学生超越前沿模型预测。语境性审计中，Phase 1.3各组的校正canonical CbD degree均为0；有用信号不是残余语境性，而是直接影响与构念耦合。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一个本体约束的本地模型构建机制和一个企业智能体路由治理诊断框架，但证据不支持可部署性、安全性、优越性、统计等价性，也不支持基于正语境性的路由规则。

Abstract: Regulated financial institutions operating under data-residency rules need tenant-owned language models that can run inside the institution's perimeter. This paper combines two related FAOS studies into one mechanism-and-control article. First, it reports a reduced-power proof-of-mechanism study of ontology-amplified distillation: a Qwen3.6-27B student is adapted to the Foundation AgenticOS ontology through supervised fine-tuning on frontier-teacher trajectories and ontology-grounded direct preference optimization (DPO), trained locally on a single Apple M5 Max from 47 synthetic, English-language, cross-domain preference pairs. On 40 held-out Vietnamese financial-domain tasks, the distilled student grounds 36 of 40 tasks (grounded rate 0.90; mean ontology term-coverage r_onto = 0.95 on a metric floored at 0.50), equal to the GPT-5 frontier baseline, which also grounds 36 of 40. The outcome is underpowered to establish equivalence: the paired-difference 95% confidence interval spans +/-4 tasks, and the run does not test or show the pre-registered amplification prediction that the student should exceed the frontier. Second, the paper consolidates a contextuality-audit method for enterprise-agent routing. In a separate negative-results pilot, the corrected canonical Contextuality-by-Default degree is zero for all Phase 1.3 groups in both the local-Qwen run and an explicitly labeled Gemma replication check; the useful signal is direct influence and construct coupling, not surviving residual contextuality. Together, the studies pair an ontology-grounded model-building mechanism with a governance diagnostic for deciding when apparent disagreement should trigger prompt standardization, multi-agent synthesis, or human review. The evidence supports neither deployability, safety, superiority, statistical equivalence, nor a contextuality-positive routing rule.

</details>


### [158] [GRID: Grammar-Railed Decoding for Enterprise SQL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11951)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GRID，一种面向企业 SQL 生成的语法约束解码引擎，通过基于解析器配置的精确 next-token mask，实现语法有效性、角色/模式约束、可审计性与近似恒定的逐 token 开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型虽能生成 SQL，但企业部署要求输出不仅像 SQL，还必须语法合法、遵守角色与 schema 策略、具备可证明保证、长序列生成时不显著变慢，并保留合规级审计记录。

Method: GRID 将 exact next-token mask 缓存在解析器配置上，即 lexer 扫描状态与 LALR(1) 栈，而非 token 序列；用增量 LALR(1) 解析器作为 viable-prefix oracle。通过字节级 trie 将 LLM token 映射到语法终结符，并区分上下文无关/上下文相关部分以保证 cache-key soundness。RBAC 被编译进语言层：角色投影裁剪产生式，schema 词典限制标识符终结符。

Result: Rust 实现的逐 token mask 中位耗时为 3.6–6.7 微秒，在两个 tokenizer 上 p50 与 p90 优于 llguidance，且零 false reject；n=16000 时 guard 成本随位置基本平坦。Spider 上，约束解码使 0.5B 模型执行准确率提升 13 个百分点；对 mask 无法强制的列级策略残差进行一次 checker-guided repair 后，7B 模型达到 94.5% executable。哈希链逐 token 审计轨迹可 bit-identical 重放，并实现 100% 篡改检测。

Conclusion: GRID 表明基于解析器配置的语法约束解码可为企业 SQL 生成提供强语法与访问控制保证、高效解码和可审计性；但其能力边界包括无法保证分布忠实性、无法单靠 mask 处理列级 RBAC，以及不适用于非 LALR(1) 语言。

Abstract: Large language models can write SQL, but enterprise deployment demands more than plausible text: outputs must be syntactically valid, must respect per-role and per-schema policy, must carry provable (not best-effort) guarantees, must not slow down as generations grow, and must leave a compliance-grade record of every decision. We present GRID (Grammar-Railed Decoding), a grammar-constrained decoding engine that keys exact next-token masks on parser configurations (lexer scan state x LALR(1) stack) rather than on token sequences, and uses the incrementally advanced LALR(1) parser itself as a viable-prefix oracle. LLM tokens are bridged to grammar terminals by a byte-level trie walk with a context-independent/context-dependent split that makes cache-key soundness hold by construction. Role-based access control is compiled into the language: role projections subset the grammar's productions and schema lexicons restrict identifier terminals, so forbidden verbs and identifiers are unreachable at mask level. Four guarantees (soundness, completeness, termination, and near-constant per-token cost) are stated with explicit preconditions and each paired with a test or benchmark. Rust kernels bring the per-token mask to a 3.6-6.7 us median, ahead of llguidance at p50 and p90 on two tokenizers with zero false rejects; per-token guard cost is position-flat at n=16,000. On Spider, constrained decoding is worth +13 execution-accuracy points at 0.5B, and one checker-guided repair pass over the provably mask-unenforceable residue (column-level policy) lifts a 7B model to 94.5% executable. A hash-chained per-token audit trail replays bit-identically with 100% tamper detection. We state plainly what the mask cannot do (distribution faithfulness, column-level RBAC, non-LALR(1) languages) and where measured cost remains.

</details>


### [159] [Calibration-First Reward-Component Auditing for Reinforcement Learning Control in Smart Greenhouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11959)
*Yuhui Bie,Guowei Xu,Yaojun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以校准为先的温室强化学习奖励审计框架，用于分解并比较智能温室控制策略在不同场景下的奖励组成与控制行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一的模拟器总回报不足以解释温室控制策略；种植者和控制工程师需要知道策略何时加热、补充 CO2、通风、控湿、使用遮阳/保温屏或补光，并希望这些评价能在仿真训练、设施适配、真实日志和规则蒸馏之间保持一致。

Method: 在 GreenLight-Gym 中将标量奖励分解为有命名的条件温度、CO2、湿度与水汽压亏缺、屏幕控制以及执行器代理项；对 GreenLight 模型进行校准，使其适配第二届 Autonomous Greenhouse Challenge 的气候日志轨迹；并在真实温室日志数据上用相同奖励组件进行评分。

Result: 框架能够在仿真、设施适配 rollout、竞赛日志数据和执行器规则蒸馏之间对同一组奖励组件进行可复现、可比较的审计。

Conclusion: 该工作强调温室强化学习评估应从单一回报转向可解释、可校准、跨场景一致的奖励分解审计，以提升智能温室控制策略的可诊断性和工程可用性。

Abstract: Greenhouse reinforcement learning can test climate-control ideas at a speed and scale that is difficult to achieve with crop experiments alone. For smart-greenhouse control, however, a single simulator return is not enough: a grower or control engineer also needs to know when the policy heats, enriches CO2, vents, manages humidity, deploys screens, or uses lamps.We propose a reproducible calibration-first reward audit framework that keeps named greenhouse-control reward components comparable across simulator training, facility-adapted rollouts, logged Autonomous Greenhouse Challenge records, and actuator-rule distillation. In GreenLight-Gym, the framework decomposes the scalar reward into conditional temperature, CO2, humidity and vapor-pressure-deficit, screen, and actuation-proxy terms; adapts GreenLight to the second Autonomous Greenhouse Challenge logged climate traces; and scores the same components on logged greenhouse data.

</details>


### [160] [Optimization Is Not All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11977)
*Minh Hua,Rita Raley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将GPT-2到更流畅后继模型的演进解读为“优化文化”的最新表现，批判以可测指标替代价值判断的语言治理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在反思大语言模型对“合法语言”标准的塑造：通常被视为工程进步的对齐、评测与优化流程，是否实际上继承并强化了一种以指标、审计和预设目标支配价值判断的文化逻辑。

Method: 论文采用概念分析与谱系追溯的方法，沿技术栈考察预训练、解码、偏好调优、基准测试和交互界面，并将其放入“审计社会”等历史脉络中理解。

Result: 分析指出，优化程序能够衡量生成文本的概率异常性或相对指标表现，却无法判断这种异常究竟是错误还是创造；尽管如此，这些程序已在短时间内获得了制定语言规范的实际权威。

Conclusion: 论文认为，语言判断的权威正从传统的语法、教育与考试制度转移到损失函数、奖励模型、基准和系统提示等技术装置；这些装置执行判断职能，却缺乏真正的判断能力。

Abstract: In 2019, OpenAI released two million GPT-2 outputs-ungrammatical, half broken-to aid the detection of machine-generated text. The alignment that produced their more fluent successors is usually regarded as an engineering achievement; we read it instead as the newest expression of optimization culture: the conviction, older than the technology, that measurable improvement along predefined axes exhausts the question of value. Tracing that conviction through the stack-pretraining, decoding, preference tuning, benchmarking, interface-and back through its genealogy in the audit society, we arrive at the limit: an optimization procedure can measure how improbable a piece of generated text is; it cannot tell whether that unlikelihood is error or invention. A procedure that cannot make that distinction has nonetheless, within half a decade, assumed the authority to set the protocols of legitimate language. Held for centuries by academies and schoolrooms, grammars and examiners, this authority has been given over to loss functions, reward models, benchmarks, and system prompts: an apparatus that executes the office of judgment with no capacity for judging.

</details>


### [161] [LP Mining with LP2Graph: A Use Case for Railway Rescheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11980)
*Jörn Maurischat,Nikola Bešinović,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LP Mining 与 LP2Graph，将已发表的 LP/MILP 模型结构转化为可复现的数据集和客观分类体系，用于支撑铁路重调度模型的自动化开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路重调度等优化领域大量依赖 MILP，但相关建模知识分散在众多论文中，符号体系不兼容；传统综述多按术语或主观叙述分类，难以复现模型结构，也无法形成可验证、可扩展的系统化知识库。

Method: 作者提出 LP2Graph：先将论文中的 LP/MILP  formulation 抽取为统一的规范模型，再表示为带类型的变量—方程图；之后对变量、约束、目标函数和整体模型结构进行自底向上的同源化与聚类，并结合应用领域和求解方法分类；分类标签由规则种子驱动并可自更新的分类器生成。

Result: 该方法产生了关于变量、约束和模型类型的客观、可重复分类体系。作者还通过将每个聚类代表重新生成为独立 LaTeX 模型，并使用 CBC、HiGHS 和 Gurobi 与原论文报告的最优值对照求解，验证了表示方法的有效性。

Conclusion: LP Mining/LP2Graph 为从文献中系统挖掘优化模型结构提供了确定性、可复现的方法，并为 raiLPminer 系列铁路重调度自动建模工具奠定了原则性基础。

Abstract: Like many optimization-driven domains, railway rescheduling relies on Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP), yet the field's modeling knowledge is scattered across hundreds of papers in incompatible notations, and narrative surveys organize it subjectively: they classify models by vocabulary rather than by structure, and reproduce neither. We present LP Mining with LP2Graph, a method that mines the structure of published LP and MILP formulations into a reproducible dataset and an induced taxonomy. Its core, LP2Graph, represents each formulation admitted by its canonical grammar as a typed variable--equation graph derived from a single canonical model; once a source is extracted into that model, everything downstream is deterministic. Each source is parsed into this model, homologized, and clustered bottom-up (over variables, then constraints and the objective, then whole-model structure) and, separately, by application domain and solution approach; the resulting groups are labeled by a rule-seeded, self-updating classifier. We validate the representation rather than assume it: per-cluster representatives are regenerated as independent LaTeX and re-solved across CBC, HiGHS and Gurobi against the optimum reported in the source paper. The outcome is an objective, repeatable taxonomy of variables, constraints and model types: the principled foundation on which our raiLPminer line of automated railway-rescheduling model development builds.

</details>


### [162] [Designing Agent-Ready Websites for AI Web Agents: A Framework for Machine Readability, Actionability, and Decision Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12056)
*Said Elnaffar,Farzad Rashidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“agent-ready website”电商网站设计框架，使网站更适合AI浏览器代理理解、验证并执行购物任务；实验显示其显著提升代理任务成功率并降低交互成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着线上购物逐渐转向由AI代理代替用户搜索、比较、约束评估和执行部分购买流程，传统面向人类的网页设计、SEO与GEO指标不足以衡量网站对代理交互的支持能力，因此需要新的设计与评估框架。

Method: 论文构建包含代理可解释性、代理可执行性和代理决策可靠性三大维度的框架，并以机器可读性、语义清晰度、动作可执行提示、上下文决策可靠性信号等特征支撑。通过受控实验比较同一电商原型的人类导向版本与agent-ready版本，保持商品目录、价格、库存和购物流程一致；使用GPT-4.1、Gemini-2.5 Flash、Grok-4 Fast三种浏览器代理，在5类任务上共运行300次，评估PASS/PARTIAL/FAIL、严格与功能成功率、错误模式、步骤数和token消耗。

Result: agent-ready版本在150次运行中获得134次PASS，基线为74次；严格成功率为89.3%对49.3%。提升最明显的任务包括商品详情提取、商品比较和多约束选择。PARTIAL结果从43次降至3次，平均步骤数从9.31降至6.49。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，通过增强结构清晰度、动作提示、证据信号和时效性指标，电商网站可以显著提高AI浏览器代理的可靠性与效率。

Abstract: Online shopping is increasingly shifting toward a model in which AI agents independently search for products, compare options, evaluate constraints, and carry out parts of the purchasing process for users. Website design must now support both human and agent-mediated interaction. This paper introduces the agent-ready website, a design framework for enhancing the readability, interpretability, verifiability, and actionability of e-commerce platforms for AI agents. Existing web design, SEO, and generative engine optimization (GEO) metrics do not fully assess a website's capacity for agent-mediated interaction. The proposed framework is structured around three dimensions agent interpretability, agent executability, and agent decision reliability supported by features such as machine readability, semantic clarity, agent actionability, and contextual decision-reliability signals. The framework is evaluated through a controlled experiment comparing a human-oriented baseline and an agent-ready version of an identical website prototype, with identical catalogs, pricing, stock, and shopping workflows. The evaluation involved five tasks, three browser-agent models (GPT-4.1, Gemini-2.5 Flash, and Grok-4 Fast), and 300 runs, measuring PASS,PARTIAL,FAIL outcomes, strict and functional success rates, error patterns, step counts, and token consumption. The agent-ready website achieved 134 PASS runs out of 150 versus 74 out of 150 for the baseline (strict success rates of 89.3% vs. 49.3%), with the largest gains in product detail extraction, comparison, and multi-constraint selection. It also reduced PARTIAL outcomes from 43 to 3 and lowered the average step count from 9.31 to 6.49. These results provide preliminary evidence that enhanced structural clarity, action cues, evidence signals, and temporal validity indicators can substantially improve the reliability and efficiency of AI browser agents.

</details>


### [163] [Graph Feedback Controls Consensus and Clique Formation in Open-Weight Language-Model Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12077)
*Samer Saab,Chaouki Abdallah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体语言模型群体中交互路由方式如何影响命名游戏中的约定形成，发现保留历史记忆与桥接式路由有助于达成共识，而基于相似性阈值的同质性路由会加剧碎片化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体语言模型系统越来越依赖局部交互路由，但运行时交互图常被视为实现细节，缺乏对其如何影响群体约定形成、行为共识与潜在状态空间共识的系统分析。

Method: 作者在1.1B到32B参数规模的开放权重语言模型群体中使用命名游戏协议；通过限制首 token 分数到 tokenizer-safe 标签，测量提示条件下的分数状态分布，构建状态相似图，并区分采样标签一致性与潜在状态空间共识；进一步比较保留记忆、同质性阈值相似路由、状态组件桥接与标签分歧桥接等干预。

Result: 保留伙伴标签证据是修复碎片化的必要但不充分条件；同质性阈值相似路由会删除跨 basin 暴露并放大碎片化；桥接式路由在有记忆时常能修复碎片化。混合四模型网格中，阈值相似路由在189次设置-随机种子运行中均未达成最终行为或状态共识，而状态组件和标签分歧桥接在18次保留记忆运行中有14次恢复最终行为共识。Qwen2.5-32B 在18个保留历史的充分混合设置中均达到稳定行为与最终状态共识。

Conclusion: 多智能体语言模型系统的运行时交互图并非中性实现细节，而是约定形成的关键机制；保留历史与跨分歧桥接能促进共识，同质性相似路由则可能系统性导致群体碎片化。早期图能量特征可作为网格内诊断信号，且结论在状态阈值、群体规模和词汇规模变化下保持稳健。

Abstract: Multi-agent language-model systems increasingly route local interactions, yet the runtime interaction graph is often treated as an implementation detail. We study convention formation in open-weight LM populations spanning 1.1B-32B parameters with a naming-game protocol. Restricted first-token scores over tokenizer-safe labels let us measure prompt-conditioned score-state distributions, construct state-similarity graphs, and separate sampled-label agreement from latent state-space consensus. Across controlled interventions, in the main open-weight repair grids, retained partner-label evidence is necessary but not sufficient: homophilous threshold-similarity routing deletes cross-basin exposure and amplifies fragmentation, while bridge-seeking routing often repairs fragmentation when memory is available. In a three-seed mixed four-model grid, threshold-similarity produces no final behavioral or state consensus in 189 setting-seed runs, whereas state-component and label-disagreement bridges recover final behavioral consensus in 14/18 retained-memory runs. Across homogeneous model populations, retained history generally shifts fragmented dynamics toward consensus; the clearest case is Qwen2.5-32B, which reaches stable behavioral and final state consensus in all 18 retained-history well-mixed settings, while threshold-similarity reaches neither form of consensus in 189 settings. Robustness over state thresholds, population size, and vocabulary size preserves the qualitative ordering, and early-window graph-energy features provide useful within-grid diagnostics.

</details>


### [164] [Operationalising Multi-Dimensional Evaluation for Conversational Agents: A Scalable, Governed Pipeline with Selective Re-evaluation and Model Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12085)
*Niranjan Kumar M,Balaji Nagarajan,Karthik Nair,Faysal Satter,Nithin Surendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GenAI Evaluation：一个面向零售对话系统的大规模、可治理、配置驱动的 LLM 评测流水线，用于评估意图对齐、事实性、帮助性、清晰度、语气及翻译质量等维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于词面重叠的指标难以衡量零售对话代理的真实质量；LLM-as-a-judge 虽具可扩展性，但在生产环境中面临治理、可复现性、成本、模式一致性、可追踪性与可靠性等挑战。

Method: 系统处理生产聊天日志，包含规范化、分片、异步执行和受 schema 约束的 LLM 打分；通过选择性重评仅处理未完成、格式错误或 schema 无效记录；采用 schema 锁定、版本化配置、验证日志和记录级溯源支持审计。

Result: 该框架每日处理约 50,000 条记录，累计评估超过 200 万次交互；基于 12,980 条分层随机人工标注数据验证，覆盖 14 类意图、156 类子意图、18 个主领域和 129 个子领域；宏平均 F1 达 0.93，翻译人类可接受性准确率为 89%。

Conclusion: GenAI Evaluation 展示了 LLM 评测方法在零售对话系统生产级部署中的可行性，通过工程化治理与可审计设计，在规模、可靠性和质量评估一致性之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Evaluating retail conversational agents requires methods beyond lexical-overlap metrics to assess intent alignment, factuality, helpfulness, clarity, tone, and overall response quality. Although LLM-as-a-judge methods provide scalable alternatives to human evaluation, production deployment introduces challenges in governance, reproducibility, cost, schema consistency, traceability, and reliability. We present GenAI Evaluation, a governed, configuration-driven pipeline for large-scale evaluation of retail conversational systems. It processes production chatbot logs through normalization, sharding, asynchronous execution, and schema-constrained LLM scoring. The framework evaluates helpfulness, truthfulness, clarity, tone alignment, and translation-specific dimensions. Selective re-evaluation processes only incomplete, malformed, or schema-invalid records, while schema locking, versioned configurations, validation logs, and record-level provenance support auditability. The framework processes approximately 50,000 records daily and has evaluated more than two million interactions. Validation used 12,980 stratified-random human-labeled records from four trained annotators. Classification covered 14 intents, 156 sub-intents, 18 major domains, and 129 sub-domains. The pipeline achieved a macro F1 score of 0.93 and 89% human-acceptability accuracy for translation.

</details>


### [165] [Representing and Generating Levels Over Time through Playtrace Reconstructive Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12097)
*Emily Halina,Matthew Guzdial*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向时间动态的游戏关卡表示“cake”，并基于该表示设计Playtrace Reconstructive Partitioning（PRP）生成算法；在Sokoban中，相比六种先进PCG方法，PRP能生成有效关卡且保持解法多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序化内容生成（PCG）方法常使用静态或高度抽象的关卡表示，难以保留电子游戏随时间展开的动态体验信息。作者希望构建一种领域无关、能隐式编码动态过程的关卡表示，从而改进关卡生成质量与泛化能力。

Method: 作者提出“cake”表示，用于描述游戏关卡随时间变化的结构，并隐式包含玩法轨迹中的动态信息。在此基础上，设计Playtrace Reconstructive Partitioning（PRP）算法，通过重构和划分玩法轨迹来生成新关卡。实验在Sokoban领域中进行，并与六种最先进的PCG方法比较。

Result: 实验表明，PRP能够生成有效的Sokoban关卡，同时不会牺牲解法多样性；相较对比方法，该方法在有效性与多样性之间取得较好平衡。

Conclusion: “cake”表示比现有表示更自然地编码了游戏的隐式动态特征，使得领域无关的PRP关卡生成算法成为可能，并为动态游戏内容生成提供了新的表示与方法框架。

Abstract: Video games are a dynamic medium experienced over time. While there are many Procedural Content Generation (PCG) approaches for generating video game levels, they often use representations that abstract away this dynamic nature. In this paper, we introduce a novel, domain-independent ``cake'' representation for game levels over time which implicitly encodes dynamic information. We present a novel level generation approach Playtrace Reconstructive Partitioning (PRP) specifically developed for this cake representation. We compare against six state-of-the-art PCG approaches in the game domain of \textit{Sokoban}, and find that our approach can generate valid levels without sacrificing solution diversity. We believe our cake representation more neatly encodes the implicit dynamic nature of games compared to existing representations, which allows for our domain-agnostic level generation algorithm PRP.

</details>


### [166] [Connected by Construction: Learning Tractable Near-Tour Marginals for Traveling Salesman Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12127)
*Ke Sun,Xinyuan Zhang,Xinwu Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 C2TSP：一种面向 TSP 的端到端无监督学习框架，直接学习具有结构意义的潜在 Hamiltonian 相关对象，而非仅依赖最终解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型 TSP 方法通常评估解码或搜索后的路线质量，但模型实际学习到的往往是热图、分配矩阵、构造策略或搜索引导分数等替代表示，难以回答模型在解码前到底学到了多少 Hamiltonian 结构。

Method: 作者基于“连通性内生保证”的有根 1-tree Gibbs 分布族，构建 C2TSP。该方法从无偏 TSP 成本中学习残差边扰动，并通过隐式微分进行端到端无监督优化；同时引入平滑 Held–Karp 层修正期望度平衡，并使用证书引导的 sharpening 机制将连通分布进一步推向更接近巡回路的结构。

Result: 实验表明，C2TSP 在解码性能上表现较强，同时保留了可解释的结构信息。消融实验验证了边扰动和证书引导 sharpening 能共同改善路线代价与 tour-like 结构。

Conclusion: 该工作强调将 TSP 学习目标从替代空间转向结构化潜变量空间，有助于在保持高质量求解性能的同时提升模型对 Hamiltonian 结构的可解释学习能力。

Abstract: Learning-based methods for the traveling salesman problem (TSP) are often evaluated through the tours produced after decoding or search, but the learned object itself frequently lives in a surrogate space such as heatmaps, assignments, construction policies, or search-guidance scores. This hides the fundamental question: what Hamiltonian structure has actually been learned before decoding? In this study, we directly answer this question by learning TSP through a structurally meaningful latent object, rather than leaving most of the Hamiltonian structure to the final decoding stage. Based on a connected-by-construction rooted $1$-tree Gibbs family, we propose an end-to-end unsupervised learning pipeline called \emph{C2TSP}. The pipeline learns residual edge perturbations from unbiased TSP cost through implicit differentiation. For structural correction, a smoothed Held--Karp layer restores expected degree balance, while certificate-guided sharpening further pushes the connected distribution toward more tour-like structures. Experiments show that C2TSP yields strong decoding performance while preserving interpretable structural information. Ablations further verify that edge perturbation and certificate-guided sharpening jointly improve both tour cost and tour-like structure.

</details>


### [167] [The Emerging Paradigm of Geospatial Foundation Models: From Pre-Training to Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12177)
*Shelley Cazares*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述并界定地理空间基础模型（GeoFMs），说明其通过大规模预训练与任务端适配，降低遥感/航拍影像智能分析门槛，并展望由大语言模型编排GeoFM工具的智能地理空间推理范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星与航空影像分析正因基础模型而发生范式转变。传统方法往往需要针对具体任务收集标注数据并从头训练或深度定制模型，成本高、周期长且难以泛化。本文旨在阐明GeoFMs如何让大规模预训练由模型提供方承担，而领域专家只需通过微调、提示或其他适配方式完成任务落地，从而兼顾先进性能、成本效率与下游任务数据安全。

Method: 文章采用概念综述与框架构建的方法：首先定义GeoFMs及其预训练范式；其次区分不同技术路线带来的能力差异，包括基于自监督学习/掩码自编码的可微调视觉模型，以及基于对比学习的视觉-语言模型；然后讨论GeoFMs运营化中的性能-成本权衡与MLOps生态；进一步提出模型适配策略分类法和面向领域专家的选择框架；最后提出由大语言模型作为编排器、调用GeoFMs完成复杂分析流程的Agentic Geospatial Reasoning愿景。

Result: 论文归纳出GeoFMs带来的核心变化：预训练与任务适配的职责分离；总结了不同GeoFM类型对应的能力边界，例如可微调模型适合任务特定优化，视觉-语言模型支持零样本、开放词汇影像分析；提出了根据任务需求、成本、性能和部署约束选择适配策略的实用框架；并将GeoFM的应用从单纯感知任务扩展到面向自然语言查询和自动化工作流的认知型地理空间推理。

Conclusion: GeoFMs有望成为遥感与地理空间AI的重要基础设施，使领域专家能够更快速、低成本且安全地构建任务系统。未来方向不仅是提升单个模型的感知能力，还包括通过大语言模型协调多种GeoFM工具，实现从影像理解到高层任务推理与自动化决策支持的转变。

Abstract: The analysis of satellite and aerial imagery has entered a new era with the advent of foundation models. This paper describes the concept of Geospatial Foundation Models (GeoFMs), which are artificial intelligence/machine learning (AI/ML) models pre-trained on massive geospatial datasets through varied methodologies. We first articulate the core paradigm shift that GeoFMs enable: a separation of duties, where large-scale model providers perform the computationally intensive pretraining, allowing domain experts to rapidly fine-tune or prompt these models for specific, mission-critical tasks. This approach democratizes access to state-of-the-art AI/ML while maintaining the security and confidentiality of the downstream task. We then explore the novel capabilities unlocked by different types of GeoFMs, distinguishing between the finetunable vision models produced by self-supervised techniques like masked auto-encoding, and the vision-language models produced by contrastive learning which enable zero-shot tasks like open-vocabulary image analysis. Next, we discuss the practical considerations for operationalizing GeoFMs, from performance-cost analysis to the broader MLOps ecosystem. To that end, we introduce a taxonomy of model adaptation strategies and propose a framework for domain experts to select the most cost-effective adaptation approach for their particular mission set. Finally, we present a forward-looking vision of Agentic Geospatial Reasoning, where Large Language Models act as intelligent orchestrators, leveraging GeoFMs as tools to answer high-level user queries in natural language and automate complex analytical workflows, moving the field from perception to cognition.

</details>


### [168] [Cost-Governed RAG: Unified Per-Tenant Cost Attribution Across Retrieval and Generation in Multi-Tenant LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12188)
*Navnit Shukla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Cost-Governed RAG，将 TurboVec 向量索引与多租户 LLM 治理网关结合，实现嵌入、检索与生成成本的租户级联合归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级 RAG 中，LLM 生成成本可按 token 计量，但检索层的向量存储、相似度计算和嵌入调用通常作为共享成本存在，导致多租户之间成本不可见和交叉补贴，缺乏有效治理。

Method: 论文设计三层成本模型，并构建 Cost-Governed RAG 架构：使用 codebook-oblivious 的 TurboVec 向量索引，利用其确定性的闭式内存公式进行近精确的租户级检索成本计算；同时通过多租户 LLM 治理网关统一记录 embedding、retrieval 和 generation 的遥测与成本归因。系统部署在 Snowpark Container Services 的云数据平台治理边界内。

Result: 在 100 个模拟租户、1000 万向量、租户规模服从对数正态分布的实验中，系统达到 99.96% 的端到端成本归因准确率，遥测开销低于查询延迟的 0.04%。在文中设定的定价假设下，相比托管向量数据库服务，检索基础设施成本降低 3.1–9.0 倍。

Conclusion: Cost-Governed RAG 表明，确定性、codebook-oblivious 量化索引可支持多租户 RAG 中精细且近精确的检索成本归因，并可能降低成本与减少共享 codebook 带来的信息泄漏面；但安全性相关结论仍属探索性，需要结合论文限制进一步验证。

Abstract: Enterprise Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) deployments face a critical governance gap: while LLM generation cost is metered per token, the retrieval layer - vector memory, similarity compute, and embedding API calls - remains an unattributed shared cost, enabling invisible cross-subsidization among tenants. We present Cost-Governed RAG, an architecture that integrates a codebook-oblivious vector index (TurboVec) with a multi-tenant LLM governance gateway, creating a unified observability stack where embedding, retrieval, and generation costs are jointly attributable per tenant. The architecture exploits TurboVec's deterministic, closed-form memory formula to enable near-exact per-tenant retrieval cost calculation - a property unavailable in graph-based indexes with non-linear memory overhead. Deployed on Snowpark Container Services within a cloud data platform's governance boundary, the system achieves 99.96% end-to-end cost attribution accuracy across 100 simulated tenants (10M vectors, log-normal size distribution) with telemetry overhead below 0.04% of query latency. The architecture reduces retrieval infrastructure cost by 3.1-9.0x compared to managed vector database services under the pricing assumptions detailed in Section IV. We formalize a three-layer cost model and demonstrate that codebook-oblivious quantization enables deterministic per-tenant cost attribution while also removing the shared-codebook leakage surface present in trained quantizers - the latter observation being exploratory and subject to the limitations described in Section VII.

</details>


### [169] [A Threshold Exceedance Framework for CBRN Uplift Evaluation in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12200)
*Rahul Gupta,Abhinav Mohanty,Payal Motwani,Venkatesh Saligrama,Satyapriya Krishna,Connor Harris,Gary Anthony Ackerman,Brandon Behlendorf,Tom Hobson,Theodore Wilson,Spyros Matsoukas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: As frontier language models advance, policymakers and model developers need methods for assessing whether model access materially increases a non-expert actor's ability to plan high-consequence Chemical, Biological, Radiological, or Nuclear (CBRN) misuse relative to public tools alone. Existing CBRN evaluations differ in non-expert definitions, threat scope, baselines, scoring rubrics, and decision rules, making results difficult to compare across studies. We introduce a Threshold Exceedance Criteria (TEC) framework that decomposes an uplift study into independently executable components: determining non-expert participant eligibility, defining the CBRN threat scope for the study, and statistically estimating material uplift. We then operationalize the TEC framework in a large-scale empirical study using a design that determines two forms of uplift: generative (where a model assists plan creation from scratch) and revisionist (where a model assists refinement of an existing plan). The study produced attack plans across the CBRN domains, which we evaluated through subject-matter-expert review to estimate generative and revisionist uplift. Applying the framework, our empirical study revealed domain heterogeneity: under this controlled pre-release evaluation, model-assisted plans sometimes received expert-equivalent instructional ratings, but confirmed material uplift was limited to the radiological domain. These findings informed mitigation and deployment-governance decisions rather than characterizing deployed model behavior. We conclude with methodological lessons for future CBRN uplift evaluations, emphasizing prespecified criteria, explicit baselines, separation of generative and revisionist estimates, and careful distinction between preliminary screening signals and confirmed risk determinations.

</details>


### [170] [Good Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12217)
*Ivan Bercovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出高质量任务的判定标准：正确、可解、可验证、规范明确，并且因真实复杂性而具有挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务设计常存在表述含糊、不可验证、脱离实践或难度来源不合理等问题，影响评估与执行质量。因此需要明确什么样的任务才是“好任务”。

Method: 作者以原则性归纳的方式给出任务质量标准：任务应贴近真实实践问题，使用从业者熟悉的语言描述，并通过测试验证最终结果而非限定解决路径。

Result: 形成了一组可操作的任务设计准则，包括正确性、可解性、可验证性、明确性、合理难度、现实相关性和结果导向测试。

Conclusion: 优秀任务应模拟真实专业场景，允许多样化解决方案，同时通过结果验证确保任务完成质量；其难度应来自问题本身的有意义复杂性，而非表述缺陷或人为限制。

Abstract: Good tasks are correct, solvable, verifiable, well-specified, and hard for interesting reasons. The best tasks describe a real problem an experienced practitioner would recognize, in language a practitioner would use, with tests that verify the outcome rather than the approach.

</details>


### [171] [Rethinking the Evaluation of Harness Evolution for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12227)
*Yike Wang,Huaisheng Zhu,Zhengyu Hu,Yige Yuan,Zhengyu Chen,Shakti Senthil,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Yulia Tsvetkov,Pradeep Dasigi,Teng Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视LLM智能体自动化harness evolution的评估方式，指出现有协议可能将搜索带来的收益误归因于harness设计，并存在对公开基准过拟合的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常用单元测试反馈迭代搜索harness配置，并在同一公开基准上报告最终性能；这会导致两个问题：一是未与同等反馈和推理预算下的简单测试时搜索基线公平比较，二是搜索集与评估集重合可能造成基准过拟合。

Method: 作者在Terminal-Bench 2.1上，使用GPT-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6，对自动harness evolution、简单test-time scaling方法和discovery基线进行对比，并控制反馈与推理预算；同时在held-out任务上评估演化得到的harness以检验泛化能力。

Result: 实验显示，自动harness evolution并不能稳定优于简单的test-time scaling方法，且在未见任务上的泛化能力有限。

Conclusion: 论文认为自动harness evolution的有效性仍需重新审视，并强调需要更公平的评估协议和更合适的自动harness设计基准。

Abstract: We revisit the evaluation of automatic harness evolution for LLM agents. Existing harness evolution methods use unit test cases to search for harness configurations and then report final performance on the same public benchmark. This protocol raises two fundamental concerns. First, harness evolution is itself an iterative search procedure that repeatedly evaluates and revises candidate harnesses using task feedback. As in agentic test-time scaling, it should therefore be compared with simple task-level search baselines under matched feedback and inference budgets to determine whether its gains arise from improved harness design or from additional search alone. Second, because the search and the final evaluation share the same benchmark, the reported gains risk overfitting to that specific task set. To address these concerns, we conduct an extensive evaluation comparing harness evolution with simple test-time scaling and discovery baselines under comparable feedback and inference budgets, and also evaluate evolved harnesses on held-out tasks to assess whether the discovered improvements generalize. Experiments on Terminal-Bench 2.1 with GPT-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.6 show that automatic harness evolution does not consistently outperform simple test-time scaling methods and exhibits limited generalization. Our results raise important questions about the effectiveness of automatic harness evolution and highlight the need for fairer evaluation protocols and benchmarks for automatic harness design. Our code is available at https://github.com/rethinking-harness-evolution.

</details>


### [172] [On-Device Deep Research at 4B: Exposure Bounds Faithfulness, Retrieval Bounds Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12257)
*Vinay Kumar Chaganti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了在个人笔记本上运行的4B小型研究代理的引用可靠性，发现增加每篇来源的可见文本长度主要提升“被引声明忠实性”，而检索召回率主要决定“可信覆盖率”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于研究代理引用质量的评估常把引用是否支持声明与是否引用了正确来源混为一个指标；对于可部署的小模型在本地设备上的引用忠实性及其成本也缺乏测量。

Method: 研究固定一个4B生成模型运行在24GB显存/内存级别的笔记本环境中，交叉比较两类因素：生成器可见的每个来源文本长度（400字符 vs 1500字符）以及提供来源质量（金标准论文 vs 检索论文）。同时区分“被引声明忠实性”和“可信覆盖率”两个指标，并使用第二个独立评审器验证稳健性。

Result: 增加来源曝光长度显著提升忠实性：在检索来源上从0.45升至0.58，在金标准来源上从0.37升至0.58；两种来源设置在更高曝光下趋于收敛。可信覆盖率在检索来源下约为0.22，基本不受曝光影响，因为检索召回率约为0.40。增加曝光约额外消耗235个输出token。

Conclusion: 小型本地研究代理的引用质量优化应先低成本地提高每篇来源的可见内容长度，以改善声明-来源忠实性；随后主要通过提升检索召回率来改善是否引用正确来源的覆盖问题。

Abstract: On-device research agents search a corpus, read sources, and write a cited brief on a personal laptop. Whether their citations are faithful, and at what cost, is unmeasured for a deployable small model. This study fixes one 4B generator on a 24 GB laptop and asks what makes its citations faithful. It separates two quantities usually reported as one number. Cited claim faithfulness asks whether the cited source supports the claim. Trustworthy coverage asks whether the agent also cites the right sources. The study crosses how much of each source the generator sees, 400 against 1500 characters, with the quality of the sources supplied, gold papers against retrieved papers. Two levers fall out, and they act on different outcomes. Exposure sets faithfulness. More of each source lifts faithfulness from 0.45 to 0.58 on retrieved sources and from 0.37 to 0.58 on gold sources, and the two settings converge, so faithfulness is bound by exposure, not by whether the source is correct. The exposure lift is robust to a second, independent judge; the exact convergence is tight under the primary judge and only approximate under the second. Retrieval sets coverage. Trustworthy coverage stays near 0.22 on retrieved sources at any exposure, because recall is held near 0.40, so exposure cannot fix which sources are cited. The extra exposure costs about 235 output tokens. The practical recipe is to raise per source exposure first, cheaply, and then treat retrieval recall as the only remaining lever.

</details>


### [173] [How Many Tasks Are Enough for Agent Benchmark Decisions? A Replay Analysis of Public LLM Agent Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12338)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在昂贵的智能体基准测试中，部分任务运行能否支持与完整基准一致的两两比较结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准通常在所有任务完成后比较两个智能体，但完整评测成本高，因此实践中常采用部分运行；然而仅报告任务比例无法说明部分评测是否足以复现完整评测的判断。

Method: 作者回放 SWE-bench、AppWorld 和 tau-bench 的公开任务级完整记录，定义“足够的部分预算”需同时满足：支持完整基准的决策、覆盖必需任务组、且未决比较比例不超过目标阈值，并在不同任务比例预算下评估。

Result: 所需任务比例差异显著。在 0 个百分点严格阈值与 5 个百分点预算网格下，AppWorld 在 15% 任务量首次满足全部目标，tau-bench 在 25%，SWE-bench Verified 在 90%；SWE-bench Lite 在主要覆盖规则下到 95% 仍未满足全部目标。

Conclusion: 部分评测报告不应只给出任务比例，还应明确两智能体的性能差距要求、任务选择方式、覆盖规则、决策规则以及允许保留的未决比较数量。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks often compare two agents after all tasks have run, but costly evaluations make partial runs tempting. A task fraction alone does not show whether a partial run supports the same pairwise conclusion as the completed benchmark. We study this question by replaying completed public task-level records from SWE-bench, AppWorld, and tau-bench. A partial budget counts as enough only when it supports the completed benchmark's decision, covers required task groups, and leaves no more than a target fraction of comparisons unresolved. The required task fraction varies sharply. At the strict 0 percentage point threshold on a 5 percentage point budget grid, AppWorld first meets all targets at 15 percent, tau-bench at 25 percent, and SWE-bench Verified at 90 percent; SWE-bench Lite does not meet all targets by 95 percent under the primary coverage rule. Partial-evaluation reports should state how much one agent must outperform another, how tasks are selected, what coverage rule is required, what decision rule is used, and how many comparisons may remain unresolved.

</details>


### [174] [PM-Bench: Evaluating Prospective Memory in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12385)
*Genglin Liu,Saadia Gabriel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PM-Bench，一个用于评估现代 LLM 智能体前瞻记忆能力的文本基准；结果显示即使最佳配置也仅达到 65.1% F1，说明该能力仍是显著短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体 AI 需要在执行持续任务的同时，记住并在未来特定线索或状态出现时执行延迟意图，这种前瞻记忆能力对可靠智能体至关重要，但现有评测对该能力缺乏系统、受控的诊断。

Method: 作者借鉴认知科学中的 Virtual Week 范式，构建文本化基准 PM-Bench。该基准模拟一周七天的活动过程，要求 LLM 智能体在持续完成当前任务的同时维护用户意图、判断延迟任务是否到期，并监控潜在环境变化。研究比较了八个先进 LLM 在八种智能体配置下的表现。

Result: PM-Bench 对所有模型和配置都具有挑战性。最佳方法是一个 GPT-5.4 智能体配置，但 F1 分数也仅为 65.1%。此外，不存在一种在所有模型上都占优的前瞻记忆增强策略。

Conclusion: PM-Bench 可作为一个受控测试平台，用于诊断 LLM 智能体在前瞻记忆方面的失败模式，并推动训练方法或推理时干预机制的发展，以提升智能体可靠执行未来意图的能力。

Abstract: A significant challenge in agentic AI is prospective memory: the ability to execute an intention at a specific future cue or state while other activities are ongoing. We introduce PM-Bench, a text-based benchmark for measuring prospective memory capabilities in modern LLM agents. Inspired by the Virtual Week paradigm from cognitive science, PM-Bench evaluates how well LLM agents maintain user intentions, execute delayed intentions, and monitor latent environment changes. Over the course of a simulated seven-day week, agents must continue an ongoing activity while deciding whether any deferred task is due. We compare eight state-of-the-art LLMs on PM-Bench under eight different agent configurations. PM-Bench proves challenging across all settings: the best method, a GPT-5.4 agent, reaches only 65.1\% F1 score under our evaluation. Furthermore, no single strategy for improving prospective memory dominates across models. We release PM-Bench as a controlled testbed for diagnosing these failures and developing training or inference-time interventions that support reliable prospective behavior.

</details>


### [175] [Critic Experience Bank: Self-Evolving Step-Level Confidence Estimation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12397)
*Yaopei Zeng,Congchao Wang,JianHang Chen,Nan Wang,Yurui Chang,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向LLM智能体的训练-free步级置信度估计框架，通过自演化评论器与执行后反馈记忆提升每一步行动是否有效的概率校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在外部环境中逐步行动，每个动作都会改变后续状态；错误动作可能浪费交互预算或造成不可逆后果。因此需要在执行前估计每个候选动作是否“有生产性”的校准概率。现有置信度估计多针对静态提示-回答，不充分利用动作执行后的环境反馈。

Method: 提出自演化评论器框架：每条轨迹结束后，由能观察完整执行反馈的hindsight LLM为每一步是否有助于任务推进生成伪标签；这些伪标签与对应情境存入记忆库。当相似步骤再次出现时，检索相关的成功/失败经验并放入critic提示中，辅助其给出执行前步级置信度。该方法无需训练，也不依赖真实步级标签。

Result: 在三个智能体基准和三种critic骨干模型上，该方法在所有数据集-critic组合中均取得最佳校准性能（ECE、Brier）和排序性能（AUC），相较最强训练-free基线，ECE最高降低54%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将智能体过去判断及其真实执行后果转化为可检索经验，可显著改善LLM智能体的步级置信度估计，提升执行前风险评估的可靠性。

Abstract: LLM agents act in external environments where each action changes the state that later decisions condition on, and where a single wrong step can waste interaction budget or trigger irreversible side effects long before the final failure is observed. Reliable deployment therefore requires \emph{step-level confidence estimation}: a calibrated probability that each proposed action is productive, available \emph{before} the action is executed. Existing LLM confidence estimators are designed to score a response from the given prompt, but agent confidence also depends on execution consequences: whether similar actions in similar situations actually advanced the task after the environment responded. We introduce the \method (\methodshort), a self-evolving critic framework in which an LLM critic accumulates evidence from its own past judgments and their observed consequences. After each trajectory, a hindsight LLM that sees the full execution feedback votes on whether each step was productive. The resulting pseudo-labels populate a memory bank from which related productive and unproductive experiences are retrieved into the critic's prompt whenever a similar step recurs. \methodshort requires no training and uses no ground truth step labels. Across three agent benchmarks and three critic backbones, \methodshort attains the best calibration (ECE and Brier) and ranking (AUC) in every dataset--critic combination, reducing ECE by up to $54\%$ relative to the strongest training-free baseline.

</details>


### [176] [Isolation as a First-Class Principle for LLM-Agent System Safety: Concepts, Taxonomy, Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12406)
*Huihao Jing,Wenbin Hu,Shaojin Chen,Haochen Shi,Sirui Zhang,Hanyu Yang,Changxuan Fan,Zhongwei Xie,Hongyu Luo,Wun Yu Chan,Wei Fan,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于 LLM Agent 系统安全的综述，主张将“隔离”作为核心安全原则，用边界视角统一解释提示注入、工具误用、记忆污染等失效现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM Agent 作为系统“大脑”参与工具调用、执行环境和多智能体交互，安全问题已从单纯的输入输出对齐扩展到系统行为与现实执行结果。现有研究按攻击类型、应用或基准分散组织，难以解释不同失效模式的共同结构原因及其在工作流中的传播机制。

Method: 论文提出以“隔离”为一等原则，将用户输入、工具访问、执行通道、智能体间通信以及环境上下文进行边界化分析，并构建五类边界的分类框架：用户-智能体、智能体-工具、智能体-执行、智能体-智能体、系统-环境。

Result: 该边界中心分类有助于定位隔离丧失的初始位置，分析风险如何跨边界传播，并对应识别各接口处更相关的防御机制。同时，论文总结了跨边界失效路径和当前开放挑战。

Conclusion: 未来 LLM Agent 安全研究应从孤立的攻击防御转向“按隔离构建”的系统设计，在架构层面预防跨边界污染、权限扩散和执行失控。

Abstract: The capability of LLM agents to function as the ``brain'' of a system fundamentally expands the scope of analysis beyond a standalone model. Consequently, safety is no longer only about input--output content alignment. It also concerns system behavior and real-world execution outcomes. However, the current literature is fragmented across attack types, applications, and benchmarks. This makes it hard to explain why failures such as prompt injection, tool misuse, and memory poisoning often share the same structural cause, and how they spread through an agent workflow. In this survey, we treat isolation as a first-class principle for LLM-agent system safety. By isolation, we refer to the separation of user inputs, tool access, execution channels, inter-agent communication, and environment-originated context. We organize the literature with a boundary-centric taxonomy of five boundaries: user-agent, agent-tool, agent-execution, agent-agent, and system-environment. This view helps identify where the loss of isolation first occurs, how compromise propagates across boundaries, and which defenses are most relevant at each interface.
